# Skills · W · part 80

780 entries, worst-case-analysis to wp-cli. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **worst-case-analysis**: Worst-case analysis (WCA) — extreme value tolerance analysis (EVTA/worst-case stackup), root sum square (RSS) vs. worst-case tolerance, Monte Carlo simulation for non-normal distributions, electronic WCA (voltage/current/temperature extremes, parameter drift over life), manufacturing and assembly tolerance extremes, reliability prediction under worst-case conditions, conservative bounding (1σ/2σ/3σ process capability), and MIL-STD-1543/NASA best practices for safety-critical design. ([MP-AI-20/mechanical-engineering-skills](https://github.com/MP-AI-20/mechanical-engineering-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/worst-case-analysis) · ★13)
- **worst-case-data-review**: Review an existing screen or component against the data it can actually receive, and report where it fails. Use when the user asks to stress or break a UI with data, or asks how it copes with messy, extreme, empty or missing real-world data, and the review itself is the request. Do not use while building or changing a UI (the data check of a change being built is frontend-design's own), to fix or restyle what the review finds, to explore design variants (throwaway-prototype), or for load and latency (performance-verification). ([minhtri2710/skills](https://github.com/minhtri2710/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/worst-case-data-review) · ★1)
- **worst-case-design**: Design the worst possible solution. Deliberate failure engineering to reveal hidden constraints and inversion opportunities. ([yogsoth-ai/creative-ideation](https://github.com/yogsoth-ai/creative-ideation/tree/HEAD/skills/worst-case-design) · ★3)
- **worst-case-lookup**: Take the single most severe domain/item judgment as the overall verdict, for RoB2 (3-value), ROBINS-I (5-value), or AMSTAR-2 (pre-filtered by critical-domain status before worst-case). Use this after domain-level-judgment (for RoB2/ROBINS-I) or quality-appraisal-checklist (for AMSTAR-2) has produced per-domain/item judgments — this SOP has two structurally distinct upstream callers and must identify which value domain it received before applying the matching lookup rule. QUADAS-2 never reaches this SOP; it terminates one step earlier at domain-level-judgment. ([yogsoth-ai/paper-reading](https://github.com/yogsoth-ai/paper-reading/tree/HEAD/skills/worst-case-lookup) · ★1)
- **worst-idea**: Worst Possible Idea (reverse brainstorming) — deliberately design the most terrible solutions to the problem, name the mechanism that makes each one bad, then invert those mechanisms into strong features. Use when ideas all feel timid and safe, when the team is optimizing instead of imagining, or for "worst idea", "reverse brainstorming", "what would make this worse", "design the disaster", "how would we guarantee failure". Do NOT use for analytical work like debugging, code review, or implementation tasks. ([danium/lateral-thinking](https://github.com/danium/lateral-thinking/tree/HEAD/skills/worst-idea) · ★335 · 3 copies)
- **worst-method-inversion**: Design worst possible solution → extract insights → invert. Deliberate failure design as creative catalyst. ([yogsoth-ai/creative-ideation](https://github.com/yogsoth-ai/creative-ideation/tree/HEAD/skills/worst-method-inversion) · ★3)
